Read the Next Article**/nFresh Spring Roll Wrappers
"Elevate your appetizers with our Fresh Spring Roll Wrappers, perfect for crafting light and vibrant rolls bursting with crisp veggies and aromatic herbs."
This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
09 Feb 2022
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Fresh Spring Roll Wrappers
Main Ingredients
Refined flour (maida), Salt
Cuisine
Chinese
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
2-2.30 hour
Cook time
21-25 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Mild
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients
2 cups refined flour (maida)
Salt to taste
Stuffing
1½ cups boiled and shredded chicken
2 tablespoons oil + for deep frying
1½ tablespoons finely chopped garlic
1 tablespoon finely chopped ginger
1 spring onion, chopped with the greens
1 small carrot, cut into thin strips
150 grams shredded cabbage
1 cup mixed coloured capsicum strips
1 teaspoon vinegar
1 teaspoon dark soy sauce
Salt to taste
Crushed black peppercorns to taste
Refined flour slurry for applying
Sliced spring onion greens for garnish
Schezwan sauce to serve
Method
Take refined flour in a large bowl. Add salt and 1¼ cups water little by little and mix with a wooden spoon till a smooth batter is formed. Continue to mix for 5 minutes. Cover and set aside to rest for 1½ hours.
To make the stuffing, heat oil in a non-stick wok. Add garlic and ginger and sauté for a minute.
Add spring onion bulb and sauté for a few seconds. Add carrot and cabbage and mix well. Sauté for 1-2 minutes.
Add coloured capsicum strips, vinegar, dark soy sauce, salt and crushed black peppercorns and mix well.
Add chicken and mix well. Add spring onion greens and toss. Take the pan off the heat and allow the mixture to cool.
Mix the rested batter well with your hand for 1-2 minutes.
Heat a non-stick tawa¬ on low heat. Take a big portion of the dough and spread a thin layer of the batter in a circular motion and collect the excess dough and keep it aside. Cook for 10-15 seconds, flip and cook for 1-2 seconds. Remove from heat and set aside.
Similarly, make the remaining spring roll wrappers. Use as required.
To make spring rolls, place a spring roll sheet on a worktop, place a generous portion of the stuffing in the centre. Apply some refined flour slurry on the edges, fold the sides and tightly roll it.
Heat sufficient oil in a kadai.
Gently slide in the rolls a few at a time and deep fry till golden brown and crisp. Drain on an absorbent paper.
Arrange them on a serving platter and serve hot with Schezwan sauce.

Read the Next Article**/nFried Cheese Burger
Mozzarella cheese squares rolled in spiced breadcrumbs, deep-fried and served sandwiched in burger buns 
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
09 Feb 2022
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Fried Cheese Burger
Main Ingredients
Mozzarella cheese block, Mini burger buns
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
2-2.30 hour
Cook time
11-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Mild
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients
200 grams mozzarella cheese block, cut into 2 inch squares
4 mini burger buns
1 cup bread crumbs
½ teaspoon garlic power
1 teaspoon onion powder
½ teaspoon dried mixed herbs
Salt to taste
½ teaspoon red chilli flakes
½ cup refined flour (maida)
Crushed black peppercorns to taste
Butter for toasting
Oil for deep frying
Layering
Ketchup- mayo for applying
Lettuce leaves as required
Tomato slices as required
Onion slices as required
Crushed black peppercorns to taste
Salt to taste
Pickled jalapeno slices as required
Black olives for garnish
Method
Spread bread crumbs on a plate. Add garlic powder, onion powder, dried mixed herbs, salt and red chilli flakes and mix well.
Take refined flour in a bowl. Add crushed black peppercorns, salt and pour ½ cup water and mix till well combined.
Dip the mozzarella squares into the refined flour mixture, coat with the bread crumbs mixture, and repeat the process one more time.
Place the prepared mozzarella squares into the freezer for 1-2 hours.
Slit the burger buns in half.
Heat butter in a non-stick grill pan, place the burger buns and toast for 30 seconds.
Heat sufficient oil in a kadai, gently slide in the frozen mozzarella squares and deep fry on medium heat till golden brown and crisp. Drain on an absorbent paper.
To make the burger, apply some ketchup-mayo on the burger bun base and place some lettuce leaves. Place the fried cheese and arrange tomato slice and onion rings. Sprinkle crushed black pepper corns, salt and arrange some pickled jalapeno slices over it. Arrange some more lettuce leaves on top. Apply some ketchup-mayo on the base of the remaining burger half and place it on top to make a burger.
Secure it with a toothpick and garnish with black olives. Serve immediately.

Read the Next Article**/nRagi Oats Dhokla
"Savor the wholesome goodness of our Ragi Oats Dhokla, a nutritious twist on a traditional favorite, combining the richness of ragi and oats with the tangy flavors of dhokla."
This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
09 Feb 2022
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Ragi Oats Dhokla
Main Ingredients
Finger millet (ragi) flour, Oats
Cuisine
Gujarati
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
7-8 hour
Cook time
16-20 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
1 cup finger millet (ragi) flour
½ cup oats, powdered
½ cup split skinless black gram (dhuli urad dal) flour
⅓ cup yogurt (dahi)
Salt to taste
½ teaspoon cumin powder
½ teaspoon red chilli powder
1 teaspoon ginger-green chilli paste
½ teaspoon baking soda
1 tablespoon oil + for greasing
Green chutney to serve
Tempering
2 tablespoons oil
1 teaspoon mustard seeds
2 teaspoons white sesame seeds
1-2 green chllies, slit
6-8 curry leaves
1 teaspoon sugar
Chopped fresh coriander leaves for garnish
Method
Combine together finger millet flour, oats powder, split skinless black gram flour, yogurt and 1¼ cups water and whisk till a smooth batter is formed. Cover and set aside to ferment for 6-8 hours.
Add salt, cumin powder, red chilli powder, ginger-green chilli paste and baking soda and mix well.
Brush a dhokla thali with some oil.
Add 1 tablespoon oil into the batter and mix well. Pour this into the greased thali.
Heat sufficient water in a steamer. Place the thali into the steamer basket. Cover and steam for 12-15 minutes.
Remove the thali and allow to cool slightly.
Scrape the sides of the thali and cut into squares.
To make the tempering, heat oil in a pan. Add mustard seeds and let them splutter. Add white sesame seeds, green chillies, curry leaves and sugar and mix well. Add ¼ cup water and mix well. Pour this onto the dhokla.
Garnish with coriander leaves. Serve with green chutney.

Read the Next Article**/nShegaon Kachori
A popular street food, these kachoris were first made in Shegaon and therefore the name. Eat one and you will want more and more
This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
09 Feb 2022
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Shegaon Kachori
Main Ingredients
Refined flour (maida), Salt
Cuisine
Maharashtrian
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
41-50 minutes
Cook time
31-40 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients
1½ cups refined flour (maida)
Salt to taste
3 tablespoons oil + for greasing + for deep frying
Fried green chillies to serve
Masala stuffing
1½ tablespoons fennel seeds (saunf)
1 tablespoon coriander seeds
1 teaspoon cumin seeds
1 cup gram flour (besan)
2 tablespoons oil
1½ ginger-green chilli paste
1 teaspoon red chilli powder
½ teaspoon garam masala powder
¼ teaspoon turmeric powder
Salt to taste
Method
Take refined flour in a large bowl. Add salt and oil and mix till the mixture resembles bread crumb consistency. Add ½ cup water and knead to a stiff dough. Cover with a damp muslin cloth and set aside for 10-15 minutes.
To make the masala stuffing, dry roast fennel seeds, coriander seeds and cumin seeds till fragrant. Transfer into a mortar and coarsely crush it with a pestle.
Heat oil in a non-stick pan. Add ginger-green chilli paste and mix well. Sauté for 1-2 minutes.
Add red chilli powder, garam masala powder, turmeric powder and salt and sauté for a few seconds.
Add gram flour and roast on low heat for 4-5 minutes. Add the crushed mixture and mix well. Set aside to cool completely.
Heat sufficient oil in a kadai.
To make the kachoris, grease the worktop with some oil. Take a medium sized portion of the dough and shape it into a ball. Place it on the greased worktop and roll it into a small disc. Add a portion of the masala stuffing and bring the edges to the centre and press to seal. Gently press and lightly roll into a small disc.
Gently slide in the kachoris a few at a time and deep fry on medium heat till they turn golden, crisp and lightly puffed.
Serve  with fried green chillies.

Read the Next Article**/nGreen Chana Waffle
"Transforming traditional flavors, the green chana waffle offers a crispy, savory twist to your breakfast routine."
This recipe is from FoodFood TV channel
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
09 Feb 2022
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Green Chana Waffle
Main Ingredients
Fresh green chana, Garlic
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
16-20 minutes
Cook time
16-20 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
1½ cups fresh green chana, blanched
3-4 garlic cloves
2-3 green chillies
1 medium onion
Salt to taste
½ tablespoon cumin powder
2½ tablespoons gram flour (besan)
1 teaspoon baking powder
1 tablespoon olive oil + for greasing
Microgreens for garnish
Red radish slices to serve
A few iceberg lettuce leaves to serve
Pickled beetroot pieces to serve
Garlic aioli to serve
Method
Put green chana, garlic and broken green chillies into a food processer jar.
Roughly chop onion and add.  Add salt, cumin powder and a little water and process into a coarse paste. Transfer into a large bowl.
Add gram flour and baking powder and mix well. Add olive oil and mix well.
Heat a non-stick waffle iron, grease with oil and pour some mixture and spread it evenly. Close and cook till golden and crisp. Remove and place on a serving plate. Make more waffles similarly.
Garnish with microgreens. Serve hot with radish, lettuce, pickled beetroot and garlic aioli.

Read the Next Article**/nQuinoa Chole
Chole served over a bed of spiced quinoa – a complete meal in itself. Rice is replaced with quinoa in this recipe. 
 This recipe is from FoodFood TV channel
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
28 Jan 2022
in
Recipes
Veg
New Update
Quinoa Chole
Main Ingredients
Quinoa, Chickpeas (kabuli chane)
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Main Course Vegetarian
Prep Time
7-8 hours
Cook time
31-40 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
2 cups quinoa , cooked
1 cup chickpeas (kabuli chane) , soaked overnight
2 1/2 tablespoons oil
1 1/2 teaspoons cumin seeds
1/2 medium onion , peeled
1/2 tablespoon finely chopped ginger
1 tablespoon finely chopped garlic
2 tablespoons chole masala
1 1/2 teaspoons red chilli powder
3 large tomatoes , pureed
Salt to taste
1 medium onion , finely chopped
2 green chillies
Fresh coriander sprigs to garnish
Salad to serve
Method
Heat 1½ tbsps oil in a pressure cooker, add 1 tsp cumin seeds and saute till they change colour.
Finely chop onion and add, mix and sauté well. Add ginger and garlic and mix well.
Add chole masala and mix well. Add red chilli powder, mix well and cook for 1 minute.
Add tomato puree and mix well. Drain and add chickpeas, salt and 2 cups water and mix well.
Close the cooker with its lid and cook under pressure till 8-10 whistles are released. Take it off the heat and allow the pressure to settle completely.
Meanwhile, heat remaining oil in a non-stick pan, add remaining cumin seeds and saute till they change colour. Add finely chopped onion and mix well.
Roughly chop green chillies and add to the onion mixture, add cooked quinoa and salt and toss till well combined.
Drizzle a little water over the quinoa, cover and cook on low heat for 2-3 minutes.
For serving, place a portion of quinoa on each serving plate, pour a portion of chole over it, garnish with a coriander sprig and serve hot with salad.

Read the Next Article**/nPot Roast Vegetable Stew
Medley of vegetables and mushrooms cooked together to make a wonderful stew. Vegetables cooked with whole spices and served with bread. 
 This recipe is from FoodFood TV channel
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
27 Jan 2022
in
Recipes
Veg
New Update
Pot Roast Vegetable Stew
Main Ingredients
Carrots, Potatoes
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Main Course Vegetarian
Prep Time
16-20 minutes
Cook time
26-30 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Mild
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
2 small carrots , peeled
2 medium potatoes , peeled
1 medium onion
1 medium sweet potato , peeled
1 tablespoon olive oil
1 tablespoon butter
6-8 garlic cloves
Salt to taste
Crushed black peppercorns to taste
4-5 baby corns , diagonally sliced
4-5 dried shiitake mushrooms , soaked, drained and halved
4-5 button mushrooms , halved
A few sprigs of fresh thyme
2 teaspoons refined flour (maida)
1 1/2 cups vegetable stock
1 cup mixed capsicums  triangles
Toasted bread slices to serve
Method
Cut carrots into 2 inch pieces, quarter potatoes and onion. Cut sweet potato into 2 inch pieces.
Heat olive oil in a non-stick heavy bottom pan, add butter and let it melt.
Add onion, carrots, sweet potatoes, potatoes, garlic cloves, salt, crushed black peppercorns and baby corns, mix and cook on high heat for 2-3 minutes.
Add shitake mushrooms, button mushrooms and thyme and sauté well.
Add refined flour and mix well. Sauté for 1-2 minutes. Add stock and mix well. Add mixed capsicums and mix well. Cover and cook for 15-20 minutes. Take it off the heat.
Transfer into a serving dish and serve hot with toasted bread slices.

Read the Next Article**/nValacha Birda
A traditional Maharashtrian dish, it is often cooked on special occasion. Field beans cooked with Indian spices and served. 
 This recipe is from FoodFood TV channel
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
27 Jan 2022
in
Recipes
Veg
New Update
Valacha Birda
Main Ingredients
Sprouted val, Cumin seeds
Cuisine
Maharashtrian
Course
Main Course Vegetarian
Prep Time
4-5 hour
Cook time
26-30 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
1 cup sprouted val , soaked and peeled
2 teaspoons cumin seeds
2 medium onions , peeled
3/4 cup scraped fresh coconut
5-6 garlic cloves
1/2 teaspoon turmeric powder
2 teaspoons red chilli powder
Salt to taste
2 tablespoons oil
1 teaspoon mustard seeds
1 teaspoon cumin seeds
1 teaspoon asafoetida
3 kokum petals
1 tablespoon chopped jaggery
Fresh coriander sprig to garnish
Method
Put cumin seeds, 1 quartered onion, coconut and sufficient water in a blender jar and blend well. Add garlic cloves and blend to a fine paste.
Finely chop remaining onion and put it in a large bowl. Add val, turmeric powder, red chilli powder and salt and mix well. Add the ground paste and mix well.
Heat oil in a deep non-stick pan, add mustard seeds, cumin seeds and asafoetida and let the seeds splutter and change colour.
Add the val mixture and mix well. Add 2 cups water and mix well. Cover and cook for 6-8 minutes.
Remove the lid and mix well. Add kokum petals and jaggery and mix well. Cook on medium heat for 12-15 minutes. Take it off the heat and rest for a few minutes.
Transfer into a serving bowl, garnish with coriander sprig and serve hot.

#Fresh coconut

A wholesome twist on classic gnocchi, this dish blends sweet potato, pumpkin, and sorghum flour, tossed in a fragrant garlic-herb pumpkin sauce and topped with parmesan.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
27 Jan 2022
in
Recipes
Veg
New Update
Main Ingredients
Sorghum flour (jowar ka atta), Boiled pumpkin puree
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Noodles and Pastas
Prep Time
26-30 minutes
Cook time
16-20 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients
1 1/2 cups
sorghum flour
(jowar ka atta)
2 cups boiled
pumpkin
puree
2 medium
sweet potatoes
, boiled, peeled and cut into medium sized pieces
Salt to taste
Black peppercorns to taste
3 tablespoons olive oil
2 tablespoons finely chopped
garlic
1/2 tablespoon chopped fresh mixed herbs
8-10 fresh basil leaves + for garnish
1 teaspoon red chilli flakes
20 grams parmesan cheese
Method
Take sorghum flour in a mixing bowl, add sweet potatoes and mash with a fork. Add 1 cup pumpkin puree, salt and crushed black peppercorns, and knead into a dough.
To make gnocchi, take small portions of the dough and shape them into cylinders. Flatten the edges.
Heat sufficient water in a deep non-stick deep pan and let it come to a boil. Drop in the gnocchis into the boiling water and cook till they float on the surface.
Meanwhile to make the sauce, heat oil in a shallow non-stick pan, add garlic and sauté well.
Add the remaining pumpkin puree and mix well. Strain the cooked gnocchi and add to the sauce and toss gently till they are well coated with the sauce.
Add salt and mixed herbs. Tear basil leaves and add to the gnocchi. Add red chilli flakes and gently toss on high heat.
Transfer into a serving plate, grate parmesan cheese on top and garnish with basil leaves. Serve hot.

Barley replaces Arborio rice in this risotto and the result is very tasty. Barley cooked with garlic , assorted mushrooms and served. 
 This recipe is from FoodFood TV channel
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
27 Jan 2022
in
Recipes
Veg
New Update
Barley Risotto
Main Ingredients
Barley (jau), Olive oil
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Main Course Vegetarian
Prep Time
8-10 hours
Cook time
21-25 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Mild
Level of Cooking

Veg
Ingredients
1 1/2 cups barley (jau) , soaked for 8 hours and cooked
1 tablespoon olive oil
1 tablespoon butter
6-8 garlic cloves , chopped
1 medium onion , finely choppoed
4-5 button mushrooms
1 cup white wine
7-8 dried shiitake mushrooms , soaked, squeezed and sliced
Salt to taste
50 grams processed cheese
Crushed black peppercorns  to taste

Method

Read the Next Article**/nAlmond Phirni
This recipe is specially for those who are intolerant to lactose, so almond milk is used instead of milk
 This recipe is from FoodFood TV channel
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
27 Jan 2022
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Main Ingredients
Almond milk, Rice
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Mithais
Prep Time
1-1.30 hour
Cook time
26-30 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Sweet
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients list for Almond Phirni
3 cups Almond milk
4 tablespoons Rice , soaked for 30 minutes and drained
a large pi to garnish Saffron
1/2 teaspoon Green cardamom powder
3/4 cup Jaggery , chopped
1/2 tablespoon Rose water
to garnish Blanched pistachios , slivered
Method
Heat almond milk in a deep non-stick pan and let it come to a boil. Reduce heat and let it simmer for 8-10 minutes.
Put rice in a blender jar, add ¼ cup water and blend to a coarse paste.
Add this paste to almond milk and mix well. Add a large pinch of saffron and mix well and cook till the rice is cooked and the mixture thickens.
Add green cardamom powder and mix well. Cook on low heat for 3-4 minutes, stirring constantly.
Add jaggery and mix well. Cook till the jaggery is completely dissolved. Add rose water and mix well. Take the pan off the heat.
Pour phirni into individual earthen bowls, sprinkle pistachio slivers on top and garnish with a few strands of saffron and serve chilled.

Read the Next Article**/nProtein Rich Poha
"Elevate your poha with the nutritious power of moth and moong sprouts, adding a protein-rich punch to this beloved breakfast dish."
This recipe is from FoodFood TV channel
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
26 Jan 2022
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Protein Rich Poha
Main Ingredients
Pressed rice (poha), Sprouted moth beans
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
0-5 minutes
Cook time
11-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Mild
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
1½ cups pressed rice
½ cup sprouted moth beans
½ cup sprouted green gram (moong)
Salt to taste
½ large onion, peeled
1 tablespoon oil
10-15 curry leaves
½ teaspoon cumin seeds
1 inch ginger
1 green chilli
½ teaspoon turmeric powder
½ lemon
Red radish slices for garnish
Fresh coriander sprig for garnish
Method
Heat a non-stick pan, add moth, green gram, ¼ cup water and salt. Mix well and cook till the sprouts soften.
Roughly chop onion. Heat oil in a non-stick pan, add curry leaves and cumin seeds and sauté till the seeds change colour.

Squeeze the juice of lemon into the pan, mix well and cook for 1-2 minutes.
Transfer the mixture into a serving bowl, garnish with red radish slices and coriander sprig and serve hot.

Read the Next Article**/nOats Thalipeeth
One of the most popular breakfast snacks amongst the Maharashtrian, this thalipeeth has oats flour replacing the traditional bhajanee flour
This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
22 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Oats Thalipeeth
Main Ingredients
Oats flour, Green chillies
Cuisine
Maharashtrian
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
11-15 minutes
Cook time
31-40 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
1½ cups oats flour
1 small carrot, grated
1 large onion, finely chopped
2 tablespoons chopped fresh coriander leaves
½ teaspoon cumin powder
½ teaspoon coriander powder
1 teaspoon red chili powder
¼ teaspoon turmeric powder
½ teaspoon garam masala powder
Salt to taste
2-3 green chillies, chopped
Oil for drizzling
Green chutney to serve
Method
Take oats flour in a bowl. Add carrot, onion, coriander leaves, cumin powder, coriander powder, red chili powder, turmeric powder, garam masala powder, salt and green chillies and mix well.

Place the non-stick tawa on heat and cook for 4-5 minutes on medium heat. Drizzle a little oil. Flip and cook the other side for 2-3 minutes or until golden and crisp.
Serve hot with green chutney.

 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.

Sanjeev Kapoor

Ingredients list for Sheer Payara

1 1/2 cups Milk powder
to grease Butter
1 tablespoon Rose water
1 teaspoon Green cardamom powder
1/4 cup Walnuts and almonds , chopped
to garnish Pistachios , chopped

Method
Grease a burfi tray with ghee, line with butter paper.

Read the Next Article**/nGluten Free Biscuit Khakra
These are not only for the gluten intolerant people – they are so tasty that you are sure to enjoy them
This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
17 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Gluten Free Biscuit Khakra
Main Ingredients
Amaranth (rajgira) flour, Barnyard millet (samo) flour
Cuisine
Gujarati
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
16-20 minutes
Cook time
21-25 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
1 cup amaranth (rajgira) flour
½ cup barnyard millet (samo) flour
Salt to taste
1 teaspoon dried fenugreek leaves (kasuri methi)
½ teaspoon red chilli powder
¼ teaspoon cumin powder
¼ teaspoon coriander powder
½ tablespoon ginger-green chilli paste
1½ teaspoons sugar
¼ teaspoon dried mango powder (amchur)
2½ tablespoons ghee
Method
Heat ghee in a small pan.
Take amaranth flour in a large bowl, add barnyard millet flour, salt, dried fenugreek leaves, red chilli powder, cumin powder, coriander powder, ginger-green chilli paste, sugar and dried mango powder.  Add hot ghee to the flour mixture and mix well.
Add 1 cup water and knead into a soft dough. Cover with a damp muslin cloth and set aside for 5 minutes.
Take a large portion of the dough and roll into a large thick disc. Cut out roundels with a cookie cutter.
Heat a non-stick tawa. Place a few discs on it and cook on medium heat for 2 minutes, flip and cook the other side for 2 minutes as well. Press the discs with a wooden spoon so that the discs do not puff up. Flip again and continue to press lightly. Repeat the process till the discs turn golden and crisp on both sides.
Place them on a cooling wire rack for a few minutes or till they cool down to room temperature.
Arrange them on a serving platter and serve or store in an airtight container.

Read the Next Article**/nRadish Cakes
"Indulge in the savory delight of deep-fried radish cakes, a delicious Oriental starter paired perfectly with a flavorful dip."
This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
17 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Radish Cakes
Main Ingredients
White radish, White radish
Cuisine
Oriental
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
8-10 hour
Cook time
1.30-2 hour
Serve
4
Taste
Mild
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients
1 small white radish, peeled and grated
1 medium white radish, peeled and cut into thin strips
1 small carrot, peeled and cut into thin strips
¾ cup rice flour
½ cup refined flour (maida)
¼ cup wheat starch or cornflour
Salt to taste
2 teaspoons sugar
1 teaspoon garlic powder
1 teaspoon onion powder
¼ teaspoon white pepper powder
3-4 dried shitake mushrooms, soaked and chopped
2 tablespoons oil + for deep frying
To serve
Fried chopped garlic
Spring onion greens, diagonally sliced
Fresh red chillies, diagonally sliced
Soy-ginger dip to serve
Method
Boil sufficient water in a deep non-stick pan, add white radish strips and boil for 4 minutes. Drain them and transfer into a bowl of ice cold water.
Add carrot strips to the same pan and boil for 4 minutes. Drain them and transfer into another bowl of ice cold water.
To make batter, take rice flour, refined flour, and wheat starch in a large bowl.
Add grated radish, salt, sugar, garlic powder, onion powder, and white pepper powder and 3 cups water and mix till well combined.
Heat 2 tablespoons oil in a non-stick pan. Add mushrooms and sauté on high heat for 2-3 minutes. Drain white radish strips and carrot strips and add to the pan and sauté well. Switch off the heat, add the batter and stir immediately to avoid any lump formation.
Transfer the mixture into a 6” x 4” square silicon cake mould and level out the top.
Heat sufficient water in a steamer.
Place the cake mould in the steamer and steam for 1 hour 5 minutes. Switch the heat off and allow to rest for 5 minutes.
Remove the cake mould from the steamer and allow to cool down to room temperature. Place it in the refrigerator for 6-8 hours or till completely chilled.
De-mould the cake and cut into 1 inch thick squares. These are the radish cakes.
Heat sufficient oil in a kadai. Gently slide in the squares, a few at a time, and deep-fry till golden brown and crisp. Drain on absorbent paper.
Arrange the radish cakes on a serving plate and garnish with fried garlic, spring onion greens and fresh red chillies. Serve hot with soy-ginger dip.

Whole wheat flour and jaggery made this cake healthy 
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
17 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Main Ingredients
Jaggery powder, Yogurt
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Desserts
Prep Time

Cook time
26-30 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Sweet
Level of Cooking
Easy

Veg
Ingredients list for Jaggery Cake
3/4 cup Jaggery powder
1/2 cup Yogurt
1/4 cup Oil
1 teaspoon Vanilla essence
1 cup Whole wheat flour (gehun ka atta)
1 teaspoon Baking powder
1/2 teaspoon Baking soda
a pinch   Cinnamon powder
1/4 cup Milk
Method
Preheat an oven to 180° C
Take the yogurt in a bowl, add oil, vanilla essence and jaggery powder and beat with an electric beater till the jaggery melts.
Sift the whole wheat flour, baking powder, baking soda and cinnamon powder directly into the yogurt mixture and mix and fold in. Add milk and mix gently till a smooth batter is formed.
Pour the batter into a 6 x 2 x 2 inch silicon rectangular cake mould and tap a few times.
Place the cake mould on a baking tray, keep the tray in the preheated oven and bake for 25-30 minutes.
Bring the baking tray out of the oven and allow the cake to cool completely.
De-mould and slice. Arrange the slices on a serving platter and serve.

"Enjoy a hearty breakfast with sattu pancakes, combining the wholesome goodness of sattu and whole wheat flour for a delightful morning treat."

Sattu Pancakes
Main Ingredients
Sattu, Yogurt
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
11-15 minutes
Cook time
31-40 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Sweet
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
1 cup sattu
½ cup yogurt
½ teaspoon baking soda
4 tablespoons butter + as required
½ cup jaggery powder
½ cup whole wheat flour
1 teaspoon baking powder
A pinch of salt
¼ teaspoon green cardamom powder
½ teaspoon vanilla essence
1 cup milk
Banana slices as required
Chopped almonds as required
Chopped pistachios as required
Maple syrup to serve
Method
Take yogurt in a bowl. Add baking soda and mix well. Set aside.
Heat butter in a deep nonstick pan. Add jaggery powder and cook till butter melts. Allow to cool slightly.
Sift sattu, whole wheat flour, baking powder and salt in a bowl.
Make a well in the centre, add yogurt mixture, butter -jaggery mixture, green cardamom powder, vanilla essence, milk and whisk well.
Heat a nonstick tawa. Grease with some butter, spread a ladleful of batter.
Cover and cook on medium heat for 2-3 minutes. Flip and cook on other side for 2 minutes.
Pile up the pancakes on serving plate. Arrange banana slices on top.
Sprinkle almonds, pistachios on top. Place some butter cubes in the centre.
Serve with maple syrup.

This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.

Energy Bar

Ingredients

¼ cup chopped cranberries
½ cup date paste

Read the Next Article**/nMasala Rawa Puri
These super crisp puris are made with a dough of rawa and some spice powders
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
17 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Main Ingredients
Fine semolina (barik rawa), Ginger-green chilli paste
Cuisine
Maharashtrian
Course
Breads
Prep Time
26-30 minutes
Cook time
11-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients list for Masala Rawa Puri
1 cup Fine semolina (barik rawa)
2 teaspoons Ginger-green chilli paste
1 teaspoon Red chilli powder
1 teaspoon Coriander powder
1/2 teaspoon Cumin powder
1/4 teaspoon Turmeric powder
to taste Salt
for greasi to deep fry Oil
to serve Chai
Method
Put the semolina in a mixer jar and grind to a fine powder. Transfer into a large bowl.
Add ginger-green chilli paste, red chilli powder, coriander powder, cumin powder, turmeric powder and salt and mix well.
Add ½ cup water and knead into soft dough. Cover with a damp muslin cloth and set aside for 10-15 minutes.
Grease the worktop with some oil.
To make puris, divide the dough into small equal portions and shape them into  balls. Roll out each ball, on the greased worktop, into a small thin disc.
Heat sufficient oil in a kadai.
Gently slide the discs, one at a time, into the hot oil and deep fry till they puff up and turn crisp. Drain on absorbent paper.
Arrange them on a serving platter and serve hot with chai.

Read the Next Article**/nRagi Jaggery Cookies
A nutritious twist on the classic cookie, blending wholesome ragi flour with the natural sweetness of jaggery for a delicious treat.
This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
17 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Ragi Jaggery Cookies
Main Ingredients
Ragi flour, Jaggery powder
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
26-30 minutes
Cook time
11-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Sweet
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
¾ cup ragi flour
½ cup jaggery powder
¼ cup cocoa powder
¼ teaspoon baking soda
½ cup butter
2-3 tablespoons milk
1-2 tablespoon chocolate chips
Method
Preheat the oven to 180º C. Line a baking tray with parchment paper.
Mix together ragi flour, cocoa powder and baking soda in a bowl.

Divide the mixture into small equal portions, shape them into balls and flatten them a little. Arrange them on the tray keeping them 1 inch apart. Place the chocolate chips on top.

Read the Next Article**/nChocolate Peanut Chikki
Indulge in the perfect fusion of rich chocolate, crunchy peanuts, and wholesome jaggery with our Chocolate Peanut Jaggery Chikki – a delightful treat that brings joy to both children and adults alike!  This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
17 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Chocolate Peanut Chikki
Main Ingredients
Cocoa powder, Roasted peanut powder
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Mithais
Prep Time
51-60 minutes
Cook time
11-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Sweet
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients list for Chocolate Peanut Chikki
1/2 cup cocoa powder
1 cup roasted peanut powder
1 cup chopped jaggery
1 tbsp ghee + for greasing
Method
Line a baking tray with parchment paper and grease a rolling pin with ghee.
Heat 1 tablespoon ghee in a non-stick pan, add jaggery and mix well. Let the jaggery melt completely. To test whether the syrup is ready, put a drop of it in cold water. If it forms a firm ball then the syrup is ready.
Add cocoa powder and mix well, add roasted peanuts and mix well.
Pour the mixture into the lined tray and spread it evenly with the greased rolling pin.
Cut into squares while it is still hot. Allow to cool and then break the squares.
Serve or store in an air-tight container.

Read the Next Article**/nChakna Platter
An assortment of spicy and crunchy snacks commonly enjoyed with drinks in India, featuring items like peanuts, fried lentils, chickpea flour noodles (sev), and seasoned nuts or seeds.
This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
14 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Chakna Platter
Main Ingredients
Masala peanuts, Unsalted roasted peanuts
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
31-40 minutes
Cook time
31-40 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Tangy
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
Masala Peanuts
1 cup salted roasted peanuts
½ teaspoon red chilli powder
½ teaspoon black salt
Salt to taste
1 teaspoon chaat masala
Oil for deep-frying
Wasabi Makhana
1 tablespoon oil
2 cups puffed lotus seeds (makhana)
2 teaspoons wasabi powder
Masala Papad Cones
1 papad
2 cups chana, boiled
1 medium onion, finely chopped
1 tomato, finely chopped
1 green chilli, finely chopped
½ teaspoon black salt
1 teaspoon chaat masala
Salt to taste
1 tablespoon chopped coriander
Juice of half lemon
Sev for garnish
Coriander leaves for garnish
Cheesy Nuts
1 cup cashews, whole
1 cup almonds, whole
¼ cup cheese powder
½ teaspoon black salt
Salt to taste
1 teaspoon chaat masala
Cheeseling Bhel
2 cups cheeseling
1 medium tomato, finely chopped
1 medium onion, finely chopped
½ raw mango, finely chopped
½ teaspoon black salt
Salt to taste
Juice of half lemon
1 ½ teaspoon chaat masala
1 tablespoon chopped coriander
¼ cup sev
Schezwan Chakli
1 cup rice flour
¼ cup split skinless black gram (dhuli urad dal), ground
4 teaspoons Schezwan sauce
¼ teaspoon cumin powder
¼ teaspoon red chilli powder
Salt to taste
Oil for deep frying and greasing
Method
Masala Peanuts
Heat sufficient oil in a kadai and deep-fry the peanuts till golden brown. Transfer to a bowl.
In a separate bowl add chilli powder, black salt, salt to taste, chaat masala and prepare a masala for
the peanuts.
Mix the prepared masala in the peanuts well, transfer to a cutting glass filled till top and serve.
Wasabi Makhana
Heat oil in a non-stick pan and add the makhana, toss for 2-3 minutes. Add wasabi powder and toss again.
Transfer to a cutting glass, filled till top and serve.
Masala Papad
Heat a non-stick tawa. Cut the papad into half and roast on the heated tawa until cooked.
Fold into a cone, once cooked.
Add boiled chana, onion, tomato, green chilli, black salt, chaat masala, salt to taste, chopped coriander and lemon juice in a bowl and mix well.
Fill this mixture in the prepared papad cones.
Garnish with sev and coriander leaves, place the cones in a cutting glass and serve.
Cheesy Nuts
Heat sufficient oil in a kadai and deep-fry the cashews and almonds till golden.
Drain on an absorbent paper and transfer to a bowl. Add cheese powder, black salt, salt to taste and chaat masala and mix well.
Transfer to a cutting glass filled till top and serve.
Cheeseling Bhel
Add cheeselings, tomatoes, onion, raw mango, black salt, salt, lemon juice and chaat masala in a bowl and mix well together.
Add chopped coriander and sev. Transfer to a cutting glass filled till top and serve.
Schezwan Chakli
Add rice flour, ground split skinless black gram (urad dal aata), schezwan sauce, salt to taste, cumin seeds, chilli powder and some water in a bowl.
Knead it to make soft dough.
Heat sufficient oil in a kadai. Grease the chakli press with some oil.
Put some dough, at a time, in a chakli press and press out chaklis in strips directly in the oil.
Deep-fry on medium heat till crisp. Drain on absorbent paper and cool down to room temperature.
Serve in a cutting glass.

Read the Next Article**/nCheese Kurkure
Got guests coming over for Diwali? This recipe will come in handy. Mozzarella and cheddar cheese stuffed rolls deep-fried till the outside is crisp and the cheese inside has melted. These are a definite hit at any party, especially with kids! 
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
14 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Cheese Kurkure
Main Ingredients
Mozzarella cheese, Processed cheese
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
21-25 minutes
Cook time
11-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Mild
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
3 tablespoons grated mozzarella cheese
30 grams processed cheese, cut into ½ cm small cubes
1 cup + 1 tablespoon refined flour + for coating
Salt to taste
¼ teaspoon baking soda
2 tablespoons yogurt
1 tablespoon oil + for deep-frying
1 teaspoon dried oregano
½ teaspoon red chilli flakes
Crushed black peppercorns to taste
Tomato ketchup as required
Method
Take 1 cup flour in a bowl. Add salt, baking soda, yogurt and some water and knead into a soft dough. Add oil and knead well. Set aside for 5-10 minutes.
Take mozzarella cheese in a bowl. Add processed cheese, dried oregano, chilli flakes, crushed peppercorns and salt and lightly mix.
Heat sufficient oil in a pan.
Divide the dough into equal portions, coat with some flour and roll them out to make thin discs. Cut each disc into rectangles.
Take 1 tablespoon flour in another bowl. Add 2-3 tablespoons water to make a thick slurry.
Apply some slurry on 2 sides of each rectangle. Put some cheese mixture in the center, and fold, applying some slurry on the edges to make thick rolls.
Deep-fry the prepared rolls in hot oil till golden and crisp. Drain on absorbent paper.
Line a serving platter with individual shot glasses and put some tomato ketchup in each glass.
Place one fried roll in each glass and serve hot.

Spicy potato mixture rolled into small bites, dipped in batter, coated with bread crumbs and deep fried.
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
14 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Potato Bites
Main Ingredients
Potatoes, Schezwan sauce
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
26-30 minutes
Cook time
11-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
3-4 medium potatoes, boiled, peeled and grated
2 tablespoons schezwan sauce
¼ cup mayonnaise
Crushed black peppercorns to taste
1-2 green chillies, chopped
1 teaspoon chilli flakes
1 tablespoon chopped fresh coriander
¼ cup dried breadcrumbs + for coating

Read the Next Article**/nPesto Sandwich with Grilled Vegetables
"Experience a burst of Mediterranean flavor with every bite of our pesto sandwich, where charred veggies meet the irresistible freshness of homemade basil pesto, all hugged by golden, crispy bread."
This recipe is from FoodFood TV channel
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Pesto Sandwich with Grilled Vegetables
Main Ingredients
Multigrain bread, Pesto sauce
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
16-20 minutes
Cook time
6-10 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Sweet
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
8 slices multigrain bread
Pesto
1 cup sweet basil leaves
2-3 garlic cloves
½ cup pine nuts
Salt to taste
¼ teaspoon crushed black peppercorns
4 tablespoons olive oil
¼ cup grated parmesan cheese
Grilled vegetables
1 medium green zucchini
2 medium green capsicums
Salt to taste
2 tablespoons olive oil
Assemble
2 medium firm red tomatoes
Salt to taste
¼ teaspoon crushed black peppercorns
4 processed cheese slices
Method
Halve and thinly slice zucchini vertically. Cut each capsicum into 4 vertical slices. Sprinkle a little salt on all of them.
Heat 2 tablespoons olive oil in a non-stick pan, place the vegetable slices in it in single layer and grill. When the underside is lightly browned, turn them over and grill the other side similarly.
Grind together basil leaves, garlic cloves, pine nuts, little salt, crushed black peppercorns, 4 tablespoons olive oil.
Add parmesan cheese and grind again till nearly smooth. Transfer the pesto into a bowl.
Cut tomatoes into roundels.
Spread pesto on all 8 bread slices. Place the grilled vegetables on 4 slices, topped with tomato roundels. Sprinkle a little salt and crushed black peppercorns.
Place cheese slices on the remaining 4 slices and then turn them over the vegetables and press lightly.
Cut each sandwich into 2 pieces and serve immediately.

Read the Next Article**/nPatra
An essential Guajarati snack or starter, Patra showcases the vibrancy of Guajarati culture and tastes sumptuous
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Patra
Main Ingredients
Colocassia leaves (arbi leaves), Gram flour (besan)
Cuisine
Gujarati
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
16-20 minutes
Cook time
11-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients
6-8 colocassia leaves (arbi patta)
2 cups gram flour (besan)
2-3 tablespoons jaggery powder
2 teaspoons tamarind pulp
½ teaspoon turmeric powder
1 teaspoon red chilli powder
¼ teaspoon asafoetida (hing)
1 teaspoon coriander powder
½ teaspoon cumin powder
Salt to taste
2 teaspoons ginger-green chilli paste
1 tablespoon chopped fresh coriander leaves
1 tablespoon oil
1 teaspoon mustard seeds
1 teaspoon white sesame seeds
1 tablespoon fresh scraped coconut
Method
Heat some water in a steamer. Place a perforated plate, cover and let the water boil.
Put gram flour in a bowl. Add jaggery powder, tamarind pulp, turmeric powder, chilli powder, asafoetida, coriander powder, cumin powder, salt, ginger-green chilli paste and half of coriander leaves and mix. Add some water and mix to make a thick batter.
Trim off the thick stems from the colocassia leaves.
To prepare one patra, place one leaf inverted and spread some batter evenly. Place another leaf inverted on top and apply some batter. Fold the sides, applying some batter and roll tightly. Apply some more batter on top.
Place the rolls in hot steamer, applying some batter if required, cover and steam for 15 minutes. Remove from steamer and cool.
Cut the rolls into roundels.
Heat oil in a non-stick pan. Add mustard seeds and white sesame seeds and let mustard seeds splutter.
Add the roundels, mix and cook for 1 minute. Add coconut and remaining coriander leaves, switch off heat and mix well.
Serve hot.

Two favourite items, pasta and sandwich combined into one results in lip-smackingly delicious snack. Relatively easy to make, you should not miss out on this dish.
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.

Sanjeev Kapoor

in

Course

Pasta Sandwich

Small white bread slices, Macaroni pasta

Fusion
Course

4

Ingredients list for Pasta Sandwich

6 tablespoons Salsa sauce

1 teaspoon Dried oregano
1 teaspoon Red chilli flakes
to taste Salt
as required Butter
as required Processed cheese
Method

1 teaspoon red chilli powder
½ teaspoon garam masala powder
Salt to taste
1 tablespoon chopped fresh coriander leaves
3 tablespoons breadcrumbs + for coating
Oil for shallow-frying
3-4 tablespoons mayonnaise
½ teaspoon mustard paste
Crushed black peppercorns to taste
1 tablespoon finely chopped mixed capsicum (red, yellow and green)
2 tablespoons chopped purple cabbage
Butter as required
Few iceberg lettuce leaves
4 cheese slices
8 tomato roundels
Method
To prepare tikkis, mix together raw banana, cottage cheese and green peas in a bowl. Add turmeric powder, chilli powder, garam masala powder and salt and mix well.
Add coriander leaves and 3 tablespoons breadcrumbs and mix well.
Divide the mixture into equal portions and shape into tikkis.
Spread some breadcrumbs on a plate and coat each tikki.
Heat some oil in a non-stick pan. Place the tikkis and shallow-fry from both sides on low heat till golden brown and crisp. Drain on absorbent paper.
Mix together mayonnaise, mustard paste, crushed peppercorns, mixed capsicum and purple cabbage in a bowl.
Halve the burger buns vertically.
Heat a non-stick tawa.
Apply some butter on the cut side of each burger halve, place on hot tawa with butter side downwards and toast till golden brown. Flip and toast till golden brown. Transfer on the worktop.
To prepare one burger, spread some mayonnaise mixture on the toasted base bun.  Place few torn lettuce leaves, one tikki, 2 tomato roundels and top with some mayonnaise mixture.
Place a cheese slice and cover with the toasted top bun.
Serve hot.

#Burger buns

#Cheese slices
#Eggless mayonnaise

#Garam masala powder
#Green peas
#Iceberg lettuce leaves
#Mustard paste
#Oil
#Purple cabbage
#Raw bananas

Read the Next Article**/nCHIROTE
These beautiful layered festive dessert may seem very difficult to make but let's see how easy they really are with all tips and tricks! 
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Main Ingredients
Refined flour (maida), Fine semolina (barik rawa)
Cuisine
Maharashtrian
Course
Mithais
Prep Time
31-40 minutes
Cook time
26-30 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Sweet
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients list for CHIROTE
3/4 cup Refined flour (maida)
1/8 cup Fine semolina (barik rawa)
a pinch Salt
1 tablespoon Ghee , hot
for deep-frying Oil , for greasing +
for garnish Pistachios powder
Sugar syrup
2 cups Sugar
2-3   Green cardamoms
1-2 tablespoons Milk
Paste
2 tablespoons Ghee
1/2 cup Cornflour/ corn starch
Method
Take refined flour in a parat. Add semolina, and salt and mix. Make a well in the centre and add the hot ghee and mix till the mixture comes together. Add ⅓ cup water, mix and knead into a semi-soft dough. Cover and set aside for 10-15 minutes.
To make the sugar syrup, heat sugar and 1 cup water and mix well. Break the green cardamom pods and add into the mixture. Cook till the sugar melts.
Add milk and mix. Reduce the heat, collect the scum that floats on top of the sugar syrup and discard it. Continue to cook till one string consistency. Take the pan off the heat.
To make the paste, take ghee in a bowl and mix till it is smooth. Add corn flour in batches and mix till a smooth thick paste is formed.
Divide the dough into 3 equal portions and shape each portion into a ball and press it lightly.
Grease the worktop with some oil. Place the flattened dough portion and roll it into a thin disc. This is a chapatti.
Take a chapatti on the worktop and spread a portion of the corn flour paste on top. Place the second chapatti over it. Apply a portion of the corn flour paste on it and place the third chapatti over it. Lightly roll and apply another portion of the corn flour paste over it.
Tightly roll the layered chapattis into a cylinder. Fold the ends and cut into 1 inch pieces. Roll out each piece into a small disc.
Heat sufficient oil in a kadai. Deep fry the small discs, a few at a time, till light golden brown and crisp. Drain on an absorbent paper.
Dip into prepared warm sugar syrup and drain on a wire rack. Allow the chirote to cool to room temperature.
Arrange the chirote on a serving plate. Garnish with pistachios powder and serve.
#Cornflour/ corn starch
#Fine semolina (barik rawa)
#Ghee
#Green cardamoms
#Milk
#Oil
#Pistachios
#Refined flour (maida)
#Salt
#Sugar
#Sugar syrup

Read the Next Article**/nBhajanee Chakli
Diwali is a great reason to have friends and family over for snacks and sweets! These Bhajanee Chaklis are not just popular snacks during diwali but also work as a great chakna for your card parties!
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Bhajanee Chakli
Main Ingredients
Bhajanee chakli flour, Oil
Cuisine
Maharashtrian
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
31-40 minutes
Cook time
11-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Mild
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients
500 grams bhajanee chakli flour
1 tablespoon white sesame seeds
Salt to taste
2-3 tablespoons hot oil
Oil for greasing + for deep frying
Method
Take bhajanee chakli flour in a large parat. Add white sesame seeds and salt. Make a well in the centre and add the hot oil and mix well. Add 1¼ cups water in batches and knead into a semi-soft dough.
Divide the dough into equal portions.
Grease a chakli press with some oil. Put a portion of the dough inside the chakli press till it is ¾ filled. Cover it with the lid.
Press out chaklis on to a plastic sheet.
Heat sufficient oil in a kadai. Gently slide in the chaklis a few at a time and deep fry till golden brown and crisp. Drain on an absorbent paper.
Allow the chaklis to cool completely. Store in an air tight container and serve as required.

Read the Next Article**/nGUD KESAR IMARTI
These imartis oozing with deep and earthy flavours of jaggery is something that is definetely going to impress your guests!
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Main Ingredients
Jaggery (gur), Split skinless black gram
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Mithais
Prep Time
4-5 hour
Cook time
16-20 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Sweet
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients list for GUD KESAR IMARTI
1 cup Jaggery (gur) , grated
3/4 cup Split skinless black gram (dhuli urad dal), soaked for 4-5 hours and drained
Saffron (kesar) a large pinch
3 tablespoons Cornflour/ corn starch
1 teaspoon Edible red colour powder
for deep-frying Ghee
8-10   Pistachios , blanched, peeled and slivered
Method
Boil 1 cup water in a wide non-stick shallow pan. Add jaggery, mix and cook till it melts. Continue to cook till it reaches one string consistency. Add saffron strands and mix well. Take the pan off the heat.
Transfer the drained dal in a blender jar and grind coarsely. Add 1 cup water and grind to a fine paste.
Transfer in a plate, add corn flour, red food colour powder and mix well with hands.
Transfer the mixture in jalebi cloth or a squeezy bottle.
Heat sufficient ghee in a shallow nonstick pan. Pipe out the mixture in circular motion and deep fry on medium heat till crisp. Drain and add into the warm jaggery syrup. Let them soak for 2-3 minutes.
Drain the imartis and place on a serving plate. Garnish with slivered pistachios and serve.

Read the Next Article**/nCream and Onion Chakli Sticks
Diwali without chakli is like chai without adrak! How about you try a new version of chakli with some exotic ingredients? Enjoy!
This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Cream and Onion Chakli Sticks
Main Ingredients
Milk powder, Onion powder
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
16-20 minutes
Cook time
11-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Mild
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients
1½ tablespoons milk powder
2 teaspoons onion powder
1 cup semolina (rawa)
Salt to taste
1½ tablespoons butter
¾ cup rice flour
2 tablespoons parmesan cheese powder
½ teaspoon castor sugar
Oil for deep frying
Method
Bring 1 cup water and salt to a boil in a deep pan. Add butter and and let it melt. Add semolina and mix till well combined. Cover and cook on low heat for 5 minutes.
Transfer the semolina mixture on a large plate and allow to cool to room temperature. Sprinkle ½ cup water and knead to a soft dough.
To make the spice mix, combine parmesan cheese powder, onion powder, castor sugar, salt and milk powder in a small bowl.
Transfer some of the mixture into a greased chakli press. Press in chakli sticks on a parchment paper.
Heat sufficient oil in a kadai. Gently slide in the chakli sticks a few at a time and deep fry till golden and crisp. Drain on an absorbent paper.
Transfer the chakli sticks in a bowl, sprinkle some of the spice mix and gently mix till each chakli stick is coated with the spice mix.
Arrange in a serving bowl. Serve.

Read the Next Article**/nAPRICOT JAM NANKHATAI
Here's the recipe you will definitely try out this Diwali. The flavour of apricot turns our regular nankhatai extraordinary. Serve it to your guests and let them fall in love with it!

 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Main Ingredients
Apricot jam, Refined flour (maida)
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Mithais
Prep Time
41-50 minutes
Cook time
11-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Sweet
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients list for APRICOT JAM NANKHATAI
as required Apricot jam
1 1/2 cups Refined flour (maida)
1 teaspoon Baking soda
1 teaspoon Baking powder
1/2 teaspoon Green cardamom powder
1 cup Powdered sugar
3/4 cup Ghee , melted
to sprinkle Pistachios powder
Method
Preheat an oven at 180ºC.
Take refined flour in a large shallow bowl. Add gram flour, baking soda, baking powder, green cardamom powder and powdered sugar and mix well. Make a well in the centre, add the melted ghee and rub well with your fingers till the mixture comes together.
To make the nankhatai, divide the mixture into equal portions. Shape each portion into a ball. Slightly flatten them and using your thumb, make a cavity in the centre. Place them at equidistance on a lined baking tray.
Place the baking tray in the preheated oven and bake for 6-8 minutes. Take the baking tray out of the oven and pipe out apricot jam in the prepared cavity of the nankhatais. Place the baking tray back in the oven and continue to bake for 2-3 minutes.
Bring the baking tray out of the oven and sprinkle pistachios powder. Allow the nankhatais to cool completely.
Arrange the nankhatais on a serving plate and serve as required.

Read the Next Article**/nSabudana Mathri
Khasta and savoury, these sago mathris are the best snacks not just for diwali but for tea time too!
This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Sabudana Mathri
Main Ingredients
Sago (sabudana), Ghee
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
41-50 minutes
Cook time
16-20 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Mild
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients
¼ cup soaked sago (sabudana)
3 tablespoons ghee
¾ cup refined flour (maida)
2 teaspoons coriander powder
¼ teaspoon turmeric powder
½ teaspoon coarsely crushed black peppercorns
Salt to taste
½ teaspoon cumin seeds
Oil for deep frying
Method
Heat ghee in a pan. Let it melt.
Take refined flour in a large shallow bowl. Add coriander powder, turmeric powder, crushed black peppercorns, salt, soaked sago, and cumin seeds and mix well. Add the melted ghee and mix till the mixture resembles bread crumb consistency.
Add ¼ cup water and knead into a stiff dough. Cover and set aside for 10-15 minutes.
Take small portions of the mixture and shape each portion into a ball. Lightly press each ball and prick it all over with a fork. This is a mathri.
Heat sufficient oil on low heat in a kadai. Gently slide in a few mathris and deep fry on low heat till they turn golden and crisp. Drain on an absorbent paper.
Allow the mathris to cool to room temperature before storing in an air tight container. Serve as required.

Read the Next Article**/nKAJU KHOYA BESAN LADDOO
This diwali make this decadent and rich besan laddoos to add a dose of excitement to the party!
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Main Ingredients
Cashew nuts, Khoya/mawa
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Mithais
Prep Time
31-40 minutes
Cook time
16-20 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Sweet
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients list for KAJU KHOYA BESAN LADDOO
1/2 cup Cashew nuts , coarsely powdered
2 cups Khoya/mawa , grated
1 cup Coarse gram flour (besan) (jada besan)
1/3 cup Ghee
2-3 teaspoons Poppy seeds (khuskhus/posto)
1/2 teaspoon Green cardamom powder
1 cup Powdered sugar
Method
Heat ghee in a wide non-stick pan. Add coarse gram flour and mix well. Continuously roast on low heat till it turns golden brown and fragrant.
Switch the heat off, add the grate khoya and mix it till well combined.
Add poppy seeds, green cardamom powder, and cashew nuts powder and sauté on low heat for 1-2 minutes.
Transfer the roasted mixture on to a large bowl and allow to cool to room temperature.
Add the powdered sugar and mix well with your hand. Take small portions of the mixture and tightly roll it into a ball. This is a laddoo.
Arrange the laddoos on a serving plate. Serve.

Read the Next Article**/nNamkeen Champakali
Let the flavour of these savoury champali unfold as you bite into its beutiful layers. These intricate looking snacks may look difficult to make but they are quite easy!
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Namkeen Champakali
Main Ingredients
Refined flour (maida)
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
31-40 minutes
Cook time
11-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Mild
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients
2 cups refined flour (maida)
Salt to taste
A large pinch of castor sugar
1 teaspoon red chilli powder
2 tablespoons hot oil
Oil for deep frying
Spice mix
¾ teaspoon red chilli powder
1½ teaspoons dried mango powder (amchur)
1 teaspoon dried pomegranate seeds (anardana) powder
¼ teaspoon roasted cumin powder
Salt to taste
Method
Take refined flour in a large shallow bowl. Add salt, castor sugar and red chilli powder hot oil and mix till the mixture resembles bread crumb consistency. Add ½ cup water little by little and knead into a stiff dough. Cover with a damp muslin cloth and set aside for 10-15 minutes.
Meanwhile, mix together red chilli powder dried mango powder, dried pomegranate seeds powder, roasted cumin powder and salt in a bowl.
Heat sufficient oil on low heat in a kadai.
Take a portion of the dough, roll it out into a thin roundel. Cut into small squares with the help of small cookie cutters. Make lengthwise cuts at equal distance in the middle of each square, leaving the edges intact. Apply some water on the edges. Gently pleat the cuts one over the other from one end to the other. Bring them together and press two ends to secure. Lightly twist once.
Heat sufficient oil in a kadai, gently slide in a few champakalis at a time, deep-fry on medium heat till golden. Drain on absorbent paper and immediately sprinkle some of the spice mix. Set aside to cool down.
Serve or store in an airtight container.
#Refined flour (maida)

Read the Next Article**/n5 SEEDS CHOCOLATE MAGAZ
Diwali is incomplete without making burfis but let's give some twist to our traditional besan burfis. Add some amazing seeds to it for an extra crunch and flavour and dip them in chocolate. Are you ready to impress your guests?

 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Main Ingredients
Flax seeds, Flax seeds
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Mithais
Prep Time
2.30-3 hour
Cook time
21-25 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Sweet
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients list for 5 SEEDS CHOCOLATE MAGAZ
1 tablespoon Flax seeds
1 tablespoon Flax seeds
1 tablespoon Pumpkin seeds
1 tablespoon White sesame seeds
1 tablespoon Melon seeds (magaz)
1 tablespoon black sesame seeds (kale til)
as required Dark chocolate , melted
as required Milk chocolate , melted
2 cups Gram flour (besan)
1/2 cup Ghee
3/4 cup Powdered sugar
1/2 teaspoon Green cardamom powder
1/4 teaspoon Nutmeg powder
Method
Heat ghee in a kadai. Add the gram flour and mix well. Continue to roast on low heat for 10-15 minutes or till the gram flour turns golden brown and fragrant.
Transfer the roasted mixture on to a large plate and allow to cool slightly.
Meanwhile, dry roast flax seeds, pumpkin seeds, white sesame seeds, melon seeds and black sesame seeds for 2-3 minutes in a non-stick pan. Take the pan off the heat.
Add powdered sugar, green cardamom powder and nutmeg powder into the roasted gram flour mixture and mix till well combined. Transfer this mixture into a greased burfi tin and spread it evenly. Smoothen the top and sprinkle the roasted seeds and lightly press. Keep it aside till it sets.
Cut the magaz into squares and gently demould. Dip half of each square alternatively in melted dark chocolate and melted milk chocolate. Set aside to set.
Arrange the magaz on a serving plate. Serve.

Read the Next Article**/nPaneer Pakora
Crisp, crunchy batter fried chunks of paneer sprinkled with chaat masala and served with some onion rings and green chutney! A plate of these with masale wali chai feels heavenly
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Paneer Pakora
Main Ingredients
Cottage cheese (paneer), Oil
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
16-20 minutes
Cook time
6-10 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
250 grams cottage cheese (paneer), cut into 1 inch cubes
Oil for deep-frying
1 cup gram flour (besan)
½ cup yogurt
1 tablespoon ginger-garlic paste
1 tablespoon chopped fresh coriander leaves + for garnishing
1 teaspoon carom seeds (ajwain)
2 teaspoons red chilli powder
Salt to taste
Chaat masala for sprinkling
Onion rings for serving
Lemon wedges for serving
Fresh mint sprig for garnishing
Green chutney for serving
Method
Heat sufficient oil in a kadai.
Combine gram flour, yogurt, ginger-garlic paste, 1 tablespoon coriander leaves, carom seeds, chilli powder and salt in a bowl. Add some water and whisk to make a semi-thick and smooth batter.

Place pakoras on one side of a serving platter, place onion rings and lemon slice and mint sprig on the other side.
Garnish with some coriander leaves and serve hot with green chutney.

Read the Next Article**/nPaneer Cutlets
These delicious paneer cutlets are perked up with loads of flavour, a favourite among youngsters and adults alike as they are appealing in both taste and appearance
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Paneer Cutlets
Main Ingredients
Cottage cheese (paneer), Potato
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
21-25 minutes
Cook time
6-10 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
200 grams cottage cheese (paneer), grated
1 medium potato, boiled, peeled and grated
½ cup soya granules, soaked for 10 minutes and drained
¼ cup grated processed cheese
1 tablespoon ginger-garlic paste
1-2 green chillies, finely chopped
1 teaspoon red chilli powder
½ teaspoon garam masala powder
1 teaspoon coriander powder
½ teaspoon cumin powder
¼ teaspoon turmeric powder
Salt to taste
Oil for deep-frying
½ cup semolina
2-3 tablespoons panko breadcrumbs
½ cup refined flour slurry
Tomato ketchup for serving
Method
Mix together cottage cheese, potato, soya granules, processed cheese, ginger-garlic paste, green chillies, chilli powder, garam masala powder, coriander powder, cumin powder, turmeric powder, salt and coriander leaves in a bowl.
Divide the cottage cheese mixture into equal portions, shape into balls and lightly flatten between your palms.
Heat sufficient oil in a kadai.
Mix together semolina and panko breadcrumbs on a plate.
Dip each cutlet in slurry and coat with semolina-breadcrumb mixture.
Put the cutlets in hot oil and deep-fry till golden brown and crisp. Drain on absorbent paper.
Serve hot with tomato ketchup.

Read the Next Article**/nPaneer Biryani
Paneer Biryani is a rich, delicious recipe with an exotic medley of spices which make your taste buds go warm
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Main Ingredients
Cottage cheese (paneer), Basmati rice
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Rice
Prep Time
41-50 minutes
Cook time
31-40 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients list for Paneer Biryani
50 grams Cottage cheese (paneer) , cut into 1/2 inch cubes
3 cups Basmati rice , cooked
2 tablespoons Ghee
2 tablespoons Butter
5-6   Cloves
5-6   Black peppercorns
3-4   Green cardamoms
1 1/2 tablespoons Ginger-garlic paste
2-3   Green chillies , slit
2 medium Onions , sliced
1 cup Tomato puree
1 teaspoon Coriander powder
1/2 teaspoon Cumin powder
1/2 teaspoon Turmeric powder
1 teaspoon Red chilli powder
1 teaspoon Biryani masala
to taste Salt
1 cup Yogurt
1 cup Fried onions
1/4 cup +  tablespoons Fresh mint leaves , chopped
1/4 cup +  tablespoons Fresh coriander leaves , chopped
5-6   Baby corns , diagonally sliced
6-7   Button mushrooms , sliced
a few strands Saffron , mixed with 3-4 tablespoons ceam
1 1/2 inches Ginger , cut into thin strips
1-2   Green chillies , cut into thin strips
Method
Heat ghee and butter in a deep non-stick pan. Add cloves, black peppercorns and green cardamoms, mix and sauté till fragrant. Add ginger-garlic paste, green chillies and onions, mix and sauté till lightly browned.
Add tomato puree, mix and saute for 1 minute. Cover and cook for 3-4 minutes or till oil separates. Add coriander powder, cumin powder, turmeric powder, red chilli powder, biryani masala and salt, mix well and sauté for 2-3 minutes. Add yogurt and mix well. Cover and cook till oil separates.
Add ½ cup fried onions, ¼ cup mint leaves and ¼ cup coriander leaves and mix well. Add baby corns, button mushrooms and mix well. Add cottage cheese cubes and salt and mix well. Add 1 cup water and mix well. Cover and cook for 2-3 minutes.
Spread the rice evenly over the cottage cheese mixture. Drizzle ½ the saffron-cream mixture and sprinkle remaining fried onions, green chilli strips, remaining mint leaves, remaining coriander leaves and remaining saffron-cream mixture. Cover the pan with aluminium foil and keep the lid over it. Cook for 4-5 minutes.
Transfer onto serving platter, garnish with mint sprig and serve hot.

#Fresh coriander leaves

#Fried onions
#Ghee
#Ginger
#Ginger-garlic paste
#Green cardamoms
#Green chillies
#Onions
#Red chilli powder

#Tomato puree

Read the Next Article**/nOrange Chennar Payesh
Bengalis love their chennar payesh and orange adds a wonderful twist to it 
 This recipe is from FoodFood TV channel
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Main Ingredients
Orange drink powder, Milk
Cuisine
Bengali
Course
Mithais
Prep Time
3-3.30 hour
Cook time
41-50 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Sweet
Level of Cooking
Moderate
Others
Veg
Ingredients list for Orange Chennar Payesh
3 tablespoons Orange drink powder
2 litres Milk
1 litres Milk , to make chenna
1 1/2 tablespoons Lemon juice
400 grams Sweet condensed milk
2 tablespoons Sugar
4-5   Almonds , slivered
4-5   Pistachios , slivered
Method
Heat 2 lt milk in a deep non-stick pan till it reduces to half.
Heat 1 lt milk in another non-stick pan.
Place a muslin cloth over a strainer kept on a bowl.
When the milk in the 2nd pan comes to a boil, add lemon juice and let it curdle. Pour the curdled milk into the strainer and let all whey drain. (Reserve this whey. You can use it to knead chapatti dough or to make chenna again).
Gather the edges of the cloth together, dip in cold water kept in another bowl, till it cools down completely. Squeeze the cloth to drain all excess water away.
Add condensed milk to the reduced milk and mix well. Cook till it reduces a little more.
Place the chenna on the worktop or in a parat and mash it with the heels of your palms.
Add sugar to reduced milk and mix and continue to cook.
Take orange drink powder in a small bowl, add ¼ cup reduced milk and mix well.
When the milk reduces to the required consistency, add small portions of mashed chenna and mix well. Cook for 3-4 minutes and take it off the heat. Cool down to room temperature.
Add orange powder mixture and mix well and chill.
Pour into individual bowls and serve chilled garnished with almonds and pistachios.
#Almonds
#Lemon juice
#Milk
#Pistachios
#Sugar

Read the Next Article**/nMoongdal ke Pakode
A popular monsoon treat, Moong Dal Ke Pakode is a delectable and yummy snack which is best enjoyed, on a rainy day, with family and friends
This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Moongdal ke Pakode
Main Ingredients
Split skinless green gram (dhuli moong dal), Oil
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
2-2.30 hour
Cook time
16-20 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
1 ½ cups split skinless green gram, soaked
1 medium onion, finely chopped
¼ cup fresh coriander, finely chopped
1 inch ginger
2 -3 green chillies
A pinch of asafoetida
A pinch of baking soda
1 teaspoon rice flour
Salt to taste
Oil to deep fry
Method
Take green gram in a mixer jar. Add onions, coriander, ginger, green chillies, asafoetida, baking soda, rice flour, salt and grind to a coarse mixture.
Heat oil to deep fry.
Drop small portions of the batter into the oil and deep fry till golden brown and crunchy.Drain on absorbent paper.
Serve hot with green chutney.

Read the Next Article**/nMixed Sprouts Cups
A perfect way to include sprouts in your diet, this recipe of Mixed Sprouts Cups is not only nutritious, but equally as tasty too
 This recipe is from FoodFood TV channel
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Mixed Sprouts Cups
Main Ingredients
Mixed sprouts (moong, chana, matki), Sabziwala bhuna masala
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Snacks and Starters
Prep Time
16-20 minutes
Cook time
16-20 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
200 grams mixed sprouts (moong, chana, matki), blanched
130 grams bhuna masla
Melted butter, as required
2 spring-roll wrappers, cut into 2 inch squares
50 grams cheese
Jalapeno pepper slices, as required
Method
Preheat oven to 180° C
Put sprouts in a non-stick pan, add bhuna masala and ½ cup water and mix well. Cook till dry.
Brush melted butter all over the spring roll pieces.  Place 2-3 pieces in each silicon mould.
Put the spiced sprouts in each mould over the wrappers.
Cut cheese into thin slices and break into small pieces.
Put jalapeno slices in each mould and over that keep the cheese pieces.
Place the moulds in a tray and bake in a preheated oven for 10-12 minutes. Remove from the sprouts cup from the moulds serve hot.

#Spring roll wrappers

Read the Next Article**/nMixed Sprout Pulao
Mixed sprout pulao is a nutritious dish due to the numerous sprouts used in making this recipe. The spices and the rice make it a homely dish, a perfect comfort food
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Course
New Update
Main Ingredients
Mixed sprouts, Basmati rice
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Rice
Prep Time
11-15 minutes
Cook time
11-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Mild
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients list for Mixed Sprout Pulao
1 1/2 cups Mixed sprouts
1 cup Basmati rice , soaked
2 medium Onions , sliced
a few   Fresh coriander sprigs
3 tablespoons Ghee
1 teaspoon Cumin seeds
1 tablespoon Ginger-garlic paste
to taste Salt
1 teaspoon Red chilli powder
1/4 teaspoon Turmeric powder
1/2 teaspoon Garam masala powder
Method
Slice onions. Chop coriander leaves finely. Wash and soak the rice for 10-15 minutes.
Heat ghee in a pressure cooker, add cumin seeds and sauté till they changes colour.
Add onions, mixed sprouts, ginger-garlic paste and salt and sauté on high heat for 3-4 minutes.
Add red chilli powder, turmeric powder and garam masala powder and mix. Drain the rice and add and mix well.
Add 3 cups of water and mix. Cover and pressure cook, on medium heat, for 5-6 minutes. Open the lid when the pressure is completely released.
Transfer into a serving bowl, garnish with coriander leaves and serve hot.

Read the Next Article**/nMatar Paneer
A sabzi made regularly in most households of India, this dish is authentic and homely in every sense
 This is a Sanjeev Kapoor exclusive recipe.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
06 Dec 2021
in
Recipes
Veg
New Update
Matar Paneer
Main Ingredients
Green peas, Cottage cheese (paneer)
Cuisine
Indian
Course
Main Course Vegetarian
Prep Time
11-15 minutes
Cook time
11-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Tangy
Level of Cooking
Easy
Others
Veg
Ingredients
1 cup green peas , blanched
250 grams cottage cheese (paneer)
2 medium onions
2 medium tomatoes
2 tablespoons oil
1 teaspoon cumin seeds
1 1/2 tablespoons ginger-garlic paste
1 tablespoon coriander powder
2 teaspoons cumin powder
1 teaspoon red chilli powder
1/4 teaspoon turmeric powder
1 teaspoon garam masala pwoder
Salt to taste
Method
Roughly chop onions and tomatoes, put into a mixer jar and grind to a fine puree.
Cut the cottage cheese into medium cubes.
Heat oil in a pressure cooker, add cumin seeds and sauté till their colour changes.
Add ginger-garlic paste and onion-tomato puree, mix well and sauté on high heat for 2-3 minutes.
Add cumin powder, coriander powder, red chilli powder, turmeric powder, garam masala and salt and mix well.
Add ¾ cup water, mix, cover and cook on high heat for 4-5 minutes. Switch off the heat and open the lid when the pressure is completely released.
Switch on the heat, add cottage cheese and green peas, mix well and cook for 30-40 seconds.
Transfer into a serving bowl and serve hot.

Read the Next Article**/nNalli Nihari
Slow cooked, rich flavours and that taste which completes a festive table. A perfect dish for festive days like Eid, when food brings everyone together.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
02 Jul 2026
in
Recipes
Main Course Mutton
New Update
Main ingredients
Mutton shanks, Mutton, Mustard oil, Onions, Ginger-garlic paste, Green chilli paste, Red chilli powder, Turmeric powder, Coriander powder, Yogurt, Nihari masala, Whole wheat slurry, Screw pine water, Saffron water, Ginger, Green chillies,
Cuisine
Hyderabadi
Course
Main course mutton
Prep time
15-20 minutes
Cook time
1.30-2 hours
Serve
4
Taste
Spicy
Level of cooking
Easy
Others
Non vegetarian
Ingredients
500 grams
lamb shanks (nalli)
250 grams
mutton
, cut into 2-inch pieces on the bone
¼ cup
mustard oil
4 large
onions
, sliced
2 tablespoons
ginger-garlic paste
½ tablespoon
green chilli paste
Salt to taste
2 teaspoons red chilli powder
½ teaspoon
turmeric powder
2 teaspoons coriander powder
½ cup
yogurt
1½ tablespoons nihari masala
2-3 tablespoons whole wheat flour slurry
2 teaspoons screw pine (kewda) water
2 teaspoons saffron water
Ginger strips for garnish
Chopped
green chillies
for garnish
Khamiri roti for serving
Method
Heat mustard oil in a pan till it begins to smoke. Add onions and sauté till golden brown.
Add lamb shanks, mutton, ginger-garlic paste, green chilli paste and salt, mix well and sear on high heat for 8-10 minutes.
Add red chilli powder, turmeric powder and coriander powder and mix well.
Add yogurt and nihari masala, mix and cook till fat separates.
Add 3 cups water, mix well, cover and cook on medium heat for 40-45 minutes. Reserve some rogan in a small bowl. Add whole wheat flour slurry, mix well and cook for 5-7 minutes.
Add screw pine water and saffron water and mix well.
Transfer into a serving bowl, drizzle the reserved rogan, garnish with ginger and green chillies and serve hot with khamiri roti.

Read the Next Article**/nShahi Paneer
Originating from the royal kitchens of Mughals, Shahi Paneer is a dish made with soft cottage cheese cubes cooked in a rich and creamy gravy. Best served with paranthas or naan.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
29 Jun 2026
in
Recipes
Main Course Vegetarian
New Update
Main ingredients
Cottage cheese (
paneer
), Green cardamoms, Black cardamom, Cinnamon, Cumin seeds, Onions, Ginger-garlic-green chilli paste, Melon seeds, Cashewnuts, Fresh tomato puree, Yogurt, Turmeric powder, Red chilli powder, Garam masala powder, Coriander powder, Khoya, Butter, Fresh cream
Cuisine
Moghlai
Course
Main course vegetarian
Prep time
15-20 minutes
Cook time
20-25 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Mild
Level of cooking
Easy
Others
Vegetarian
Ingredients
300 grams
cottage cheese (paneer)
2-3 tablespoons oil
6-8
green cardamoms
1 black cardamom
½ inch
cinnamon
1 teaspoon
cumin seeds
3 large
onions
, sliced
1½ tablespoons ginger-garlic-green chilli paste
2-3 tablespoons
melon seeds (magaz)
10-15
cashewnuts
1 cup
fresh tomato puree
¼ cup
yogurt
½ teaspoon turmeric powder
1 teaspoon
red chilli powder
¾ teaspoon
garam masala powder
+ to sprinkle
1½ teaspoons coriander powder
Salt to taste
¼ cup grated khoya
4 tablespoons butter
2 tablespoons fresh cream + to drizzle
Paranthas for serving
Method
Cut cottage cheese (paneer) into 1-inch cubes.
Heat oil in a pan, add green cardamoms, black cardamom, cinnamon and cumin seeds, mix and sauté till fragrant.
Add onions and ginger-garlic-green chilli paste, mix and sauté till onions turn light golden brown.
Add melon seeds and cashewnuts, mix and sauté for 2-3 minutes.
Add tomato puree, yogurt, turmeric powder, red chilli powder, ½ teaspoon garam masala powder, coriander powder and salt, mix well and cook till oil surfaces.
Add khoya and 2 tablespoons butter and cook for 2-3 minutes.
Allow to cool slightly, transfer into a grinder jar and grind to a fine mixture.
Strain through a fine strainer directly into another pan. Add 2 tablespoons butter, cottage cheese pieces, ¼ teaspoon garam masala powder and fresh cream, mix well and cook on low heat till butter melts.
Transfer into a serving bowl, sprinkle some garam masala powder, drizzle fresh cream and serve hot with paranthas.
#Black cardamom
#Butter

Read the Next Article**/nIndian Burrito Wrap
This burrito is a Mexican-Indian fusion at its best. Made with leftover chapatis, leftover rice and leftover rajma masala, it is wholesome and comforting.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
29 Jun 2026
in
Recipes
Snack
New Update
Main ingredients
Leftover chapatis, Ghee, Cumin seeds, Cooked rice, Leftover rajma masala, Fresh coriander leaves, Mint mayo, Carrot, Cucumber, Red capsicum, Yellow capsicum, Mixed lettuce leaves
Cuisine
Fusion
Course
Snack
Prep time
20-25 minutes
Cook time
10-15 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Savoury
Level of cooking
Easy
Others
Vegetarian
Ingredients
4 leftover
chapatis
(roti)
2 tablespoons
ghee
1 teaspoon
cumin seeds
1 cup
cooked rice
½ cup leftover rajma masala
2 tablespoons chopped fresh coriander leaves
4 tablespoons
mint mayo
Carrot
strips as required
Cucumber
strips as required
Red capsicum
strips as required
Yellow capsicum
strips as required
Mixed lettuce leaves
as required
Method
Heat ghee in shallow non-stick pan, add cumin seeds and once they start to change colour, add rice, rajma masala and coriander leaves, mix well and cook for 2-3 minutes.
Heat a non-stick tawa. Place the chapatis, one at a time, and heat on both the sides for 30 seconds.
Place the chapatis on worktop and spread 1 tablespoon mint mayo evenly on each chapati. Place the rice mixture on one side and place carrot, cucumber, red capsicum and yellow capsicum strips on top.
Arrange lettuce leaves on top and roll tightly.
Cut into halves, place them on a serving platter and serve.

Read the Next Article**/nThai Peanut Noodles
Thai peanut noodles is one of the most popular dishes in the country of its origin. Soft rice noodles are served with a medley of thinly sliced vegetables coated with a rich, creamy and nutty sauce that is made with peanut butter, honey and sesame oil.
By
Sanjeev Kapoor
29 Jun 2026
in
Recipes
Noodles and Pastas
New Update
Main ingredients
Rice noodles, Peanut butter, Honey, Red chilli sauce, Dark soy sauce, Lemon, Blended sesame oil, Purple cabbage, Spring onions with greens, Cucumber, Red capsicum, Yellow capsicum, Carrot, Edmame beans, White sesame seeds
Cuisine
Thai
Course
Noodles and pastas
Prep time
25-30 minutes
Cook time
0 minutes
Serve
4
Taste
Savoury
Level of cooking
Easy
Others
Vegetarian
Ingredients
150 grams
rice noodles
¼ cup peanut butter
2 tablespoons
honey
2 tablespoons
red chilli sauce
2 teaspoons
dark soya sauce
½
lemon
2 teaspoons blended sesame oil
Salt to taste
¼ small
purple cabbage
, shredded
2 spring onion greens, thinly sliced
2 spring onion bulbs, thinly sliced
1 small green cucumber, cut into thin strips
1 small
red capsicum
, seeded and cut into thin strips
1 small
yellow capsicum
, seeded and cut into thin strips
1 small
carrot
, peeled and cut into thin strips
½ cup edamame beans
White sesame seeds to sprinkle
Method
Take rice noodles in a bowl, add sufficient hot water and set aside for 5-6 minutes.
Take peanut butter in a bowl, add honey, red chilli sauce and dark soya sauce. Squeeze lemon juice into the bowl, add blended sesame oil and salt and mix well.
Drain the rice noodles and transfer into a serving bowl. Arrange purple cabbage, spring onion greens, spring onion bulbs, green cucumber, red capsicum, yellow capsicum, carrot and edamame beans all around the noodles.
Drizzle prepared sauce mixture all over and sprinkle white sesame seeds on top.
Mix well and serve.
